Method: 提出SAM-MT，采用显式查询表示不同个体目标并与全局上下文共享表示并行处理；通过解耦掩码注意力保持个体身份区分、稀疏记忆实现稳定的时序演化，以及专门处理遮挡和防止重叠的策略。

Result: SAM-MT成功将延迟与目标数量解耦，在10个目标上达到>36 FPS的实时速度，与单目标基线相当，同时保持了SAM2鲁棒的视频分割性能。

Conclusion: SAM-MT为多目标视频分割提供了一种高效的实时解决方案，在保持高性能的同时实现了与目标数量无关的恒定延迟。

Original Abstract: Modern Video Object Segmentation (VOS) involves tracking and segmenting user-specified targets. While recent approaches have achieved remarkable performance in single-target scenarios, extending them to multi-target settings typically involves replicating the single-target processing for each individual object, resulting in reduced frame rates (FPS) with unbounded latency as target count increases. Built upon Segment Anything 2 (SAM2), we propose SAM-MT, which addresses this by transforming the model into an interactive framework for real-time Multi-Target video segmentation. SAM-MT uses explicit queries to represent different individual targets, in parallel with a shared representation for global context. It employs decoupled masked attention to keep individual identities distinct from cross-target interference, and sparse memory for stable temporal evolution, along with specialized strategies for occlusion handling and overlap prevention. SAM-MT successfully decouples latency from the number of targets, achieving real-time speed on par with single-target baselines (>36 FPS for 10 targets) while maintaining SAM2's robust video segmentation performance.

中文摘要翻译: 现代视频目标分割（VOS）涉及跟踪和分割用户指定的目标。尽管近期方法在单目标场景中取得了显著性能，但将其扩展到多目标设置通常需要为每个独立目标复制单目标处理流程，导致帧率（FPS）下降且延迟随目标数量增加而无限增长。基于Segment Anything 2（SAM2），我们提出SAM-MT，通过将模型转化为用于实时多目标视频分割的交互式框架来解决这一问题。SAM-MT使用显式查询来表示不同的个体目标，并与全局上下文的共享表征并行处理。它采用解耦掩码注意力来保持个体身份免受跨目标干扰，利用稀疏记忆实现稳定的时序演化，并配备专门的遮挡处理和重叠预防策略。SAM-MT成功地将延迟与目标数量解耦，实现了与单目标基线相当的实时速度（10个目标>36 FPS），同时保持了SAM2鲁棒的视频分割性能。

</details>


### [62] [HumanForge: A Human-Centric Deepfake Video Benchmark with Multi-Agent Forgery Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08705)
*Wenbo Xu,Zhimin Chen,Xiaojie Liang,Hengrui Liu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanForge——一个统一、大规模、多范式的以人为中心的视频伪造数据集，以及Gen2Anno——一种基于LangGraph的模块化主动多智能体管道，用于自动化数据集构建与标注。实验表明，现有检测器和多模态大模型在该数据集上存在零样本泛化和细粒度推理方面的显著挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频伪造基准主要聚焦换脸或全局文本到视频合成，忽略了以人为中心视频中的人-物交互、人-人交互以及多模态对齐等关键维度，无法满足日益逼真的人体视频伪造检测需求。

Method: 提出Gen2Anno管道，基于LangGraph构建模块化主动多智能体系统，包含六个专门智能体（源特征分析、MoE参考分析、闭环取证验证等），自动生成超1.8万段高保真视频片段及结构化对比全标注（包括二分类决策、细粒度伪影类别和时空定位）。

Result: 基于最先进传统检测器和大型多模态模型（LMMs）的广泛基准测试表明，现有方法在HumanForge上的零样本泛化和细粒度推理面临显著挑战。

Conclusion: HumanForge填补了以人为中心视频伪造检测领域缺乏多范式、细粒度标注数据集的空白，揭示了现有检测方法在该复杂场景下的局限性，为后续研究提供了基准资源。

Original Abstract: Rapid advancements in video diffusion models and temporal editing tools have enabled the generation of highly realistic human-centric videos, posing unprecedented challenges to digital content forensics. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on either face-swapping or global text-to-video synthesis, overlooking the crucial dimensions of human-object or human-human interactions and multi-modal alignment. To address these limitations, we introduce HumanForge, a unified, large-scale, and multi-paradigm human-centric video forgery dataset. To construct and annotate this dataset without labor-intensive manual labeling or hallucinated monolithic prompts, we propose Gen2Anno, a modular active multi-agent pipeline built on LangGraph. Gen2Anno coordinates six specialized agents-ranging from source profiling to MoE-based reference analysis and closed-loop forensic verification-to generate over 18K high-fidelity video segments and produce structured, contrastive omni-annotations containing binary decisions, fine-grained artifact categories, and spatio-temporal localization. Extensive benchmarks using state-of-the-art traditional detectors and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) demonstrate the significant challenges of zero-shot generalization and fine-grained reasoning on HumanForge. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型和时间编辑工具的快速发展使得生成高度逼真的以人为中心的视频成为可能，这对数字内容取证提出了前所未有的挑战。现有基准主要聚焦于换脸或全局文本到视频合成，忽略了人-物交互或人-人交互以及多模态对齐等关键维度。为解决这些局限性，我们提出HumanForge——一个统一、大规模、多范式的以人为中心视频伪造数据集。为无需繁重的人工标注或幻觉式单一提示即可构建和标注该数据集，我们提出Gen2Anno——一种基于LangGraph的模块化主动多智能体管道。Gen2Anno协调六个专门智能体——从源特征分析到基于混合专家模型（MoE）的参考分析以及闭环取证验证——生成超过1.8万段高保真视频片段，并产生包含二分类决策、细粒度伪影类别和时空定位的结构化对比全标注。使用最先进传统检测器和大型多模态模型（LMMs）的广泛基准测试表明，现有方法在HumanForge上的零样本泛化和细粒度推理面临显著挑战。代码和数据集将公开发布。

</details>


### [63] [LTM: Large-scale Terrain Model for Wildfire-prone Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08711)
*Xiao Fu,Yue Hu,Meida Chen,Peter Anthony Beerel,Barath Raghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态重建框架，利用过时的数字高程模型（DEM）作为几何先验，通过物理驱动的像素-像素对齐实现图像与DEM数据的配准，从而高效生成高保真深度图，显著提升火灾区域地形重建的精度与计算效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 野火多发区域通常覆盖广阔地域，传统三维重建方法性能不佳；机载LiDAR虽分辨率高但成本昂贵且更新频率低；基于图像的方法成本较低，但面临视觉特征稀疏和图像重叠有限的挑战。因此需要一种低成本、高效且可扩展的地形重建方案。

Method: 提出一种多模态重建框架，将过时的数字高程模型（DEM）作为几何先验用于基于图像的三维重建。核心创新在于采用物理驱动的像素-像素对齐方法，在图像与DEM数据之间实现直接对齐，从而消除了昂贵的特征匹配步骤，大幅降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在基于真实野火区域构建的大地形模拟器上进行评估，该方法在给定有姿态的图像和旧DEM数据的情况下，能够生成高保真深度图并保持实时性能。与现有技术相比，在重建精度和计算效率方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为野火应急响应提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，在保持实时性的同时实现了高保真地形重建。

Original Abstract: Accurate 3D terrain maps are essential for emergency response when assessing wildfire hazards. However, wildfire-prone regions often span vast areas where conventional reconstruction methods underperform. Airborne LiDAR systems provide high-resolution terrain data, but they are expensive and infrequently updated. Image-based methods offer a lower-cost alternative, but struggle due to sparse visual features and limited image overlap. We propose a multi-modal reconstruction framework leveraging outdated Digital Elevation Models (DEMs) as geometric priors for image-based 3D reconstruction. Our key innovation is physics-based pixel-pixel alignment between images and DEM data, dramatically reducing computational complexity by eliminating expensive feature matching procedures. To validate our approach, we developed a large-terrain simulator based on a real wildfire-prone area, generating realistic images enabling a comprehensive evaluation. Given posed images and legacy DEMs, our method produces high-fidelity depth maps while maintaining real-time performance. We find significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency over existing techniques, offering a scalable solution for wildfire response.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的三维地形图对于野火灾害评估中的应急响应至关重要。然而，野火多发区域通常覆盖广阔地域，传统重建方法在此类场景下性能不佳。机载LiDAR系统能提供高分辨率地形数据，但成本高昂且更新频率低。基于图像的方法作为低成本替代方案，却因视觉特征稀疏和图像重叠有限而效果不佳。我们提出一种多模态重建框架，利用过时的数字高程模型（DEM）作为基于图像的三维重建的几何先验。我们的关键创新在于实现图像与DEM数据之间的物理驱动像素-像素对齐，通过消除昂贵的特征匹配过程，大幅降低计算复杂度。为验证该方法，我们基于真实野火区域开发了一个大型地形模拟器，生成逼真图像以支持全面评估。在给定有姿态的图像和历史DEM数据的情况下，我们的方法能够生成高保真深度图，同时保持实时性能。我们发现，与现有技术相比，该方法在重建精度和计算效率方面均有显著提升，为野火应急响应提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

</details>


### [64] [Pose-to-Biomechanics: Bridging 3D Human Pose Estimation and Biomechanical Attribute Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08725)
*Ayda Eghbalian,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BioModule是一个轻量级即插即用的时间Transformer模块，可附加在任何3D姿态估计器下游，从标准17关节点3D骨架预测生物力学属性，实现从视觉姿态估计到生物力学运动分析的桥梁。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D人体姿态估计主要优化几何关键点精度，而康复、运动科学、工效学和临床运动分析等实际应用需要描述身体如何运动、承载和激活的生物力学量，因此需要一种方法将姿态估计扩展为物理可解释的运动分析。

Method: 提出BioModule，一个轻量级即插即用的时间Transformer模块，可附加在任何3D姿态估计器下游，无需修改上游模型。构建了Human3.6M与Human3.6Mplus对齐的大规模数据集，建立并验证了两个数据集坐标系之间的解剖对应关系，实现帧精确的跨模态监督训练。

Result: 在七种最先进的3D姿态估计器上进行了系统性基准测试，首次分析了上游姿态估计质量如何传播到下游生物力学预测保真度，结果表明BioModule可作为紧凑模块化桥梁有效工作。

Conclusion: BioModule成功将现有3D姿态估计器扩展至生物力学运动分析领域，是连接视觉姿态估计与生物力学意义人体运动分析的紧凑模块化桥梁。

Original Abstract: Recent progress in 3D human pose estimation has made markerless recovery of skeletal motion increasingly accurate and scalable. However, most pose estimators remain optimized for geometric keypoint accuracy, while many real-world applications in rehabilitation, sports science, ergonomics, and clinical movement analysis require biomechanical quantities that describe how the body moves, loads, and activates. In this work, we propose BioModule, a lightweight plug-in temporal transformer that attaches downstream of any 3D pose estimator and predicts biomechanical attributes from standard 17-joint 3D skeletons. BioModule is estimator-agnostic and requires no modification of the upstream pose model, enabling existing pose estimators to be extended toward physically interpretable motion analysis.
  To train and evaluate BioModule, we construct a large-scale aligned dataset pairing Human3.6M video and 3D keypoints with the biomechanical label space of Human3.6Mplus. We establish and verify anatomical correspondence between coordinate systems of the two datasets, enabling frame-accurate cross-modal supervision. Using this aligned supervision, BioModule predicts biomechanical quantities. We further benchmark BioModule across seven state-of-the-art 3D pose estimators, providing the first systematic analysis of how upstream pose estimation quality propagates to downstream biomechanical prediction fidelity. The results position BioModule as a compact, modular bridge between vision-based pose estimation and biomechanically meaningful human motion analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，3D人体姿态估计的进展使得无标记骨骼运动恢复日益精确且可扩展。然而，大多数姿态估计器仍针对几何关键点精度进行优化，而康复、运动科学、工效学和临床运动分析等许多实际应用需要描述身体如何运动、承载和激活的生物力学量。在这项工作中，我们提出BioModule，一个轻量级即插即用的时间Transformer模块，可附加在任何3D姿态估计器下游，从标准17关节点3D骨架预测生物力学属性。BioModule与估计器无关，无需修改上游姿态模型，使现有姿态估计器能够扩展至物理可解释的运动分析。
  为训练和评估BioModule，我们构建了一个大规模对齐数据集，将Human3.6M视频和3D关键点与Human3.6Mplus的生物力学标签空间配对。我们建立并验证了两个数据集坐标系之间的解剖对应关系，从而实现帧精确的跨模态监督。利用这种对齐监督，BioModule能够预测生物力学量。我们进一步在七种最先进的3D姿态估计器上对BioModule进行基准测试，首次系统分析了上游姿态估计质量如何传播到下游生物力学预测保真度。结果表明，BioModule是连接视觉姿态估计与生物力学意义人体运动分析的紧凑模块化桥梁。

</details>


### [65] [WaspMOT: A Benchmark for Long-Term Multi-Object Tracking of Trichogramma Wasps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08729)
*Tomasz Stanczyk,Yuan Gao,Hardik Agarwal,Seongroo Yoon,Tiantao Zhang,Vincent Calcagno,Francois Bremond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaspMOT是一个长时多目标跟踪基准，包含约12,000帧/序列的黄蜂跟踪数据。实验表明现有方法在长时身份保持上存在严重轨迹碎片化问题，即使使用完美检测器也是如此。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MOT基准以短视频序列为主，无法充分评估长时身份保持能力。为填补这一空白，作者构建了WaspMOT长时跟踪基准。

Method: 构建了包含10个序列（每序列约12,000帧）的黄蜂长时跟踪数据集，采用密集MOTChallenge标注和oracle检测；在统一协议下评估ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT和McByte五种跟踪器；提出简单空间轨迹拼接基线。

Result: 所有方法均出现显著轨迹碎片化，即便使用完美检测；空间轨迹拼接基线持续提升性能，表明现有方法仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: WaspMOT揭示了现有跟踪方法在长时身份保持上的局限，这些局限在常规数据集中无法观测到，为长时关联研究提供了新基准。

Original Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) has achieved strong performance on benchmarks dominated by short video sequences. However, such datasets do not adequately evaluate long-term identity preservation, where objects must be tracked consistently over extended durations. We introduce WaspMOT, a benchmark designed to address this gap through long-duration tracking of Trichogramma wasps in controlled ecological experiments. The dataset contains 10 sequences of approximately 12,000 frames each (over 8 minutes at 25 FPS), with dense MOTChallenge annotations and oracle detections to isolate association performance.
  Unlike existing benchmarks, WaspMOT forms a closed-set tracking scenario where all individuals remain present throughout the sequence, requiring consistent identity assignment across thousands of frames despite abrupt jumps, occlusions, and highly similar appearance. We establish a benchmark by evaluating five tracking-by-detection methods, including ByteTrack, BoT-SORT, C-BIoU, OC-SORT, and McByte, under a unified protocol. Results show that all methods suffer from significant trajectory fragmentation, highlighting the difficulty of long-term identity preservation even with perfect detections. A simple spatial tracklet stitching baseline consistently improves performance, indicating that substantial gains remain possible.
  WaspMOT provides a new benchmark for studying long-term association and reveals limitations of current tracking approaches that are not observable on conventional datasets. The benchmark will be made publicly available at the project repository: https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/ .

中文摘要翻译: 多目标跟踪（MOT）在以短视频序列为主的基准上已经取得了强劲的性能。然而，这类数据集无法充分评估长时间的身份保持能力——即目标必须在较长时间跨度内被一致地跟踪。我们提出了WaspMOT，一个旨在通过受控生态实验中赤眼蜂的长时跟踪来填补这一空白的基准。该数据集包含10个序列，每个序列约12,000帧（在25 FPS下超过8分钟），具有密集的MOTChallenge标注以及用于隔离关联性能的oracle检测。与现有基准不同，WaspMOT构成一个闭集跟踪场景，所有个体在整个序列中始终存在，需要在数千帧中面对突变跳跃、遮挡和高度相似的外观时保持一致的身份分配。我们通过在一个统一协议下评估五种基于检测的跟踪方法（包括ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT和McByte）来建立一个基准。结果表明，所有方法都遭受显著的轨迹碎片化问题，凸显了即使在完美检测条件下长时身份保持的困难性。一个简单的空间轨迹拼接基线持续提升了性能，表明仍有实质性的改进空间。WaspMOT为研究长时关联提供了一个新基准，并揭示了在常规数据集中无法观测到的当前跟踪方法的局限性。该基准将在项目仓库中公开发布：https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/。

</details>


### [66] [OpenCoF: Learning to Reason Through Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08763)
*Xinyan Chen,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Dongzhi Jiang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenCoF框架，包含OpenCoF-17K推理视频数据集和基于Wan2.2微调的Wan-CoF视频生成模型，通过时序帧间的链式推理（Chain-of-Frame）增强大模型的推理能力，并引入视觉和文本推理token来组织中间推理状态，实验表明广泛的时间监督与显式推理机制对视频推理至关重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要在通用视频语料上训练，缺乏针对链式帧推理（Chain-of-Frame）的多样化监督和专门设计，导致模型难以通过时序关联的帧来进行逻辑推理。

Method: 构建OpenCoF-17K数据集（覆盖11个任务族），在Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基础上微调得到Wan-CoF模型；进一步引入视觉推理token和文本推理token，分别捕获低层视觉线索和高层语义先验，用于空间和时间推理。

Result: Wan-CoF在四个视频推理基准上显著优于Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基线；通过注意力分析揭示了推理token在模型深度、去噪步数、空间和时间维度上的贡献机制。

Conclusion: 更强的视频推理能力需要广泛的时间监督和显式的中间推理状态组织机制。

Original Abstract: Reasoning has become a core capability for large models, especially when reliable decisions require understanding logical consequences. Recent video generation models offer a reasoning path distinct from previous Chain-of-Thought (CoT): reasoning can unfold through temporally connected frames, known as Chain-of-Frame (CoF) reasoning. However, existing video generators are primarily trained on general video corpora, still lacking diverse supervision and dedicated designs for CoF reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce OpenCoF, a framework comprising the OpenCoF-17K dataset, a reasoning video dataset spanning 11 task families, and Wan-CoF, a fine-tuned video model for studying whether diverse temporal supervision improves CoF behavior. Across four video reasoning benchmarks, Wan-CoF achieves considerable gains over the Wan2.2-I2V-A14B baseline. Building on this, we empirically explore more advanced designs for CoF capabilities, i.e., equipping the model with visual and textual reasoning tokens. This mechanism respectively captures low-level visual cues and high-level semantic priors for spatial and temporal reasoning. Through performance comparisons and attention analysis, we examine how these tokens contribute across model depth, denoising steps, space, and time. Our results suggest that stronger video reasoning requires both broad temporal supervision and explicit mechanisms for organizing intermediate reasoning state. We open-source the dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research on reasoning-oriented video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 推理已成为大模型的核心能力，尤其是在需要理解逻辑后果才能做出可靠决策的任务中。最近的视频生成模型提供了一种不同于以往链式思维（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）的推理路径：推理可以通过时序关联的帧展开，称为链式帧（Chain-of-Frame, CoF）推理。然而，现有的视频生成器主要是在通用视频语料上训练的，仍然缺乏多样化的监督和针对CoF推理的专门设计。为解决这一不足，我们引入了OpenCoF框架，包含跨11个任务族的推理视频数据集OpenCoF-17K，以及基于微调的视频模型Wan-CoF，用于研究多样化的时间监督是否能改善CoF行为。在四个视频推理基准上，Wan-CoF相比Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基线取得了显著提升。在此基础上，我们进一步探索了增强CoF能力的更先进设计，即为模型配备视觉和文本推理token。该机制分别捕获低层视觉线索和高层语义先验，用于空间和时间推理。通过性能对比和注意力分析，我们考察了这些token在模型深度、去噪步骤、空间和时间维度上的贡献方式。我们的结果表明，更强的视频推理能力既需要广泛的时间监督，也需要显式的中间推理状态组织机制。我们开源了数据集、模型和代码，以促进面向推理的视频生成领域的未来研究。

</details>


### [67] [Enhancing In-context Panoramic Generation via Geometric-aware Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08765)
*Haoran Feng,Ruiyang Zhang,Longyi Zhang,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Canvas360是一个两阶段全景图生成框架，结合几何感知预训练和下游任务微调，并提出了包含100万高质量配对样本的Canvas360Dataset，在风格迁移、修复、外扩和编辑等多任务上优于此前方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全景图生成方法缺乏大规模高质量训练数据，且缺乏统一框架以支持多种上下文感知的全景生成任务（如风格迁移、修复、外扩和编辑）。

Method: 提出两阶段框架Canvas360：第一阶段进行几何感知预训练，通过并行深度生成、速度循环填充（velocity circular padding）和相似性损失正则化来学习几何感知表示；第二阶段通过token级拼接实现统一的多任务上下文全景生成微调。同时构建了Canvas360Dataset（100万高质量配对样本）。

Result: 实验表明Canvas360在全景图像保真度上显著提升，在全景专用FAED指标上表现尤为突出，在多项定量评估中取得领先或竞争性结果。

Conclusion: Canvas360通过几何感知预训练和大规模高质量数据集，实现了统一且灵活的上下文全景生成框架，在任务覆盖和建模灵活性上超越此前方法。

Original Abstract: In this work, we present Canvas360, a two-stage framework for in-context panoramic generation that combines geometry-aware pretraining with downstream task-specific fine-tuning. To address the lack of large-scale, high-quality training data tailored to in-context panoramic tasks, we propose Canvas360Dataset, a collection of 1M high-quality paired panoramic samples for style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing, enabling effective supervision across diverse in-context generation scenarios. On the modeling side, Canvas360 enhances text-to-panorama generation through parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization, enabling the model to learn geometry-aware representations, capture object distortion details, and improve geometric consistency and global coherence. Furthermore, empowered by strong panoramic priors, Canvas360 enables a unified in-context panoramic generation framework that supports diverse downstream tasks via token-level concatenation, surpassing prior methods in both task coverage and modeling flexibility. Extensive experiments show that Canvas360 improves panoramic image fidelity, achieving particularly strong performance on the panorama-specific FAED metric and competitive or leading results across the reported quantitative evaluations. More information can be found on our project page: https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出Canvas360，一个用于上下文感知全景生成的两阶段框架，结合了几何感知预训练与下游任务特定微调。为解决上下文全景任务缺乏大规模高质量训练数据的问题，我们提出了Canvas360Dataset，包含100万高质量配对全景样本，涵盖风格迁移、修复、外扩和编辑任务，为多样化的上下文生成场景提供有效监督。在建模方面，Canvas360通过并行深度生成、速度循环填充和相似性损失正则化增强文本到全景图的生成能力，使模型学习几何感知表示、捕捉物体畸变细节，并提升几何一致性和全局连贯性。此外，凭借强大的全景先验，Canvas360通过token级拼接实现统一的上下文全景生成框架，支持多种下游任务，在任务覆盖和建模灵活性上超越先前方法。大量实验表明，Canvas360提升了全景图像的保真度，在全景专用FAED指标上表现尤其出色，并在多项定量评估中取得领先或具有竞争力的结果。更多信息请访问项目页面：https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

</details>


### [68] [OPSD-V: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Post-Training Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08766)
*Hongyu Liu,Chun Wang,Feng Gao,Xuanhua He,Yue Ma,Ziyu Wan,Yong Zhang,Xiaoming Wei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPSD-V，一种基于同策略自蒸馏的少步自回归视频扩散模型后训练范式，通过引入真实长视频作为时序上下文提供密集轨迹级监督，减少长自回归推理中的误差累积和动态退化，无需改变采样器或推理缓存机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少步自回归视频生成模型能生成长视频且延迟低，但在长自回归推理过程中存在误差累积和动态退化（运动变弱）问题，亟需一种能减少长程退化同时保留原有少步推理路径的方法。

Method: 核心思想是在训练中引入真实长视频作为时序上下文，提供密集的轨迹级监督。学生模型按照精确的推理路径生成，每个块基于自身先前生成的KV缓存进行条件生成；教师模型在相同的去噪状态下评估，但使用更干净的AR一致时序缓存（用真实视频上下文替换旧历史），从而在同策略AR缓存动态下提供密集去噪级矫正目标，不改变采样器、去噪步数或推理缓存机制。

Result: 应用于Self-Forcing和LongLive等代表性少步AR视频模型，实验显示在视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong评分上持续提升。用户研究（10人，20对视频）显示OPSD-V在66.0%的整体偏好判断中被选为更优（排除平局时为82.5%）。

Conclusion: OPSD-V能有效减少少步自回归视频扩散模型的长程退化，显著提升视频质量和运动动态，是一种通用且即插即用的后训练范式。

Original Abstract: We propose OPSD-V, an on-policy self-distillation paradigm for post-training few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. Existing few-step AR video generators can produce long videos with low latency, but still suffer from error accumulation and weakened motion dynamics during long autoregressive rollout. OPSD-V reduces long-horizon degradation while preserving the original few-step inference path. The key idea is to introduce real long-video data as temporal context during training and use it to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. Specifically, the student follows the exact inference-time rollout, generating each chunk conditioned on its own previously generated KV cache. In parallel, the teacher is evaluated at the same student-visited denoising states, but uses a cleaner AR-consistent temporal cache in which older history can be replaced by real-video context. This provides dense denoising-level corrective targets under on-policy AR cache dynamics, without changing the sampler, number of denoising steps, or inference-time cache mechanism. We apply OPSD-V to representative few-step AR video models, including Self-Forcing and LongLive. Experiments show consistent improvements in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores. A user study with 10 participants comparing 20 video pairs shows that OPSD-V is preferred over the base models in 66.0% of overall-preference judgments (82.5% excluding ties).

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出OPSD-V，一种用于后训练少步自回归（AR）视频扩散模型的同策略自蒸馏范式。现有的少步自回归视频生成器能够以低延迟生成长视频，但在长时间自回归推理过程中仍然存在误差累积和运动动态减弱的问题。OPSD-V在保留原有少步推理路径的同时减少了长程退化。其核心思想是在训练中引入真实长视频作为时序上下文，并利用它提供密集的轨迹级监督。具体而言，学生模型遵循精确的推理时自回归展开，每个块基于其自身先前生成的KV缓存进行条件生成。与此同时，教师模型在相同的学生访问过的去噪状态下进行评估，但使用更干净的、与自回归一致的时序缓存——其中较早的历史可以被真实视频上下文替换。这在同策略自回归缓存动态下提供了密集的去噪级矫正目标，无需改变采样器、去噪步数或推理时的缓存机制。我们将OPSD-V应用于代表性的少步自回归视频模型，包括Self-Forcing和LongLive。实验表明，在视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong评分上均有一致的提升。一项10名参与者比较20对视频的用户研究显示，OPSD-V在66.0%的整体偏好判断中优于基础模型（排除平局时为82.5%）。

</details>


### [69] [Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning for Panoramic Outdoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08769)
*Weijian Chen,Weibo Yao,Yuhang Zhang,Xiaolin Tang,Guo Wang,Weijun Zhang,Xitong Gao,Yihao Chen,Hongde Qin,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoLOG提出了一种面向大规模户外全景3D高斯溅射重建的两阶段粗到细框架，通过几何与梯度引导的分区策略(G²PS)解决全景图像全局可见性导致的分区退化问题，并构建了首个大规模户外全景重建基准数据集Pano360，在保证可扩展块并行训练的同时实现了最先进的渲染质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将3D高斯溅射(3DGS)扩展到大规模户外场景在数据采集和计算上成本高昂。采用等距柱状投影(ERP)的全景图像虽能通过360°视场角降低采集成本，但其全向可见性使得依赖局部相机视锥的现有分区策略失效，导致分块优化退化为全局训练，因此亟需针对全景3DGS重建设计新的分区策略。

Method: 提出PanoLOG两阶段粗到细框架：(1) 全局粗阶段：利用天空球建模和全景单目深度监督获得可靠的初始几何结构；(2) 细化阶段：提出G²PS(Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy)，通过视差驱动的不确定性构建自适应包围体，并基于梯度重要性评分分配相机。同时构建了首个大规模户外全景重建数据集Pano360。

Result: 大量实验表明，G²PS策略在保持可扩展的块并行训练能力的同时，实现了最先进的渲染质量。

Conclusion: PanoLOG通过粗到细框架和G²PS分区策略有效解决了全景图像在大规模户外3DGS重建中的分区退化问题，实现了高质量的可扩展重建。模型、训练代码和数据集均已开源。

Original Abstract: Scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to large outdoor scenes is costly in both data acquisition and computation. Adopting panoramic images with equirectangular projection (ERP) can reduce capture effort via their full $360^{\circ}$ field of view, yet the resulting omnipresent visibility invalidates existing partitioning strategies that rely on local camera frustums, causing block-wise optimization to degenerate into global training. Thus, we propose PanoLOG, a two-stage coarse-to-fine framework equipped with a Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy tailored for large-scale panoramic 3DGS reconstruction. In the global coarse stage, PanoLOG leverages sky-sphere modeling and panoramic monocular depth supervision for reliable geometry, while in the refinement stage, G$^2$PS builds adaptive bounding volumes via parallax-driven uncertainty and assigns cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Furthermore, we construct Pano360, the first benchmark on large-scale panoramic dataset for outdoor scene reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while maintaining scalable, block-parallel training. Our models, training code, and dataset are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 将3D高斯溅射(3DGS)扩展到大规模户外场景在数据采集和计算上成本高昂。采用等距柱状投影(ERP)的全景图像虽能通过其360°全视场角降低采集成本，但其全向可见性使得依赖局部相机视锥的现有分区策略失效，导致分块优化退化为全局训练。为此，我们提出了PanoLOG——一种两阶段粗到细框架，并配备了一种专为大规模全景3DGS重建设计的几何与梯度引导分区策略(G²PS)。在全局粗阶段，PanoLOG利用天空球建模和全景单目深度监督来获得可靠的几何结构；在细化阶段，G²PS通过视差驱动的不确定性构建自适应包围体，并基于梯度重要性评分分配相机。此外，我们构建了Pano360——首个面向户外场景重建的大规模全景数据集。大量实验表明，G²PS在保持可扩展的块并行训练能力的同时，实现了最先进的渲染质量。我们的模型、训练代码和数据集均已公开。

</details>


### [70] [LongE2V: Long-Horizon Event-based Video Reconstruction, Prediction, and Frame Interpolation with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08770)
*Cheng-De Fan,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Chen-Wei Chang,Chin-Yang Lin,Kun-Ru Wu,Yu-Chee Tseng,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongE2V提出了一种利用预训练视频扩散先验的方法，统一处理基于事件的视频重建、预测和帧插值任务，通过自回归展开、自适应上下文切换、重编码对齐与交叉残差校正以及事件体素密度增强等技术创新，实现了高质量、长时稳定的视频生成，在多个真实基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从稀疏事件流中恢复高质量视频极具挑战性：回归方法容易模糊纹理，而现有生成模型在长期稳定性方面表现不佳。为此，本文旨在设计一种能够同时处理事件视频重建、预测和帧插值任务，并兼具高数据效率、优异感知质量和长时间稳定性的方法。

Method: 1) 利用预训练视频扩散模型作为先验，通过微调基础视频模型实现高数据效率和优异感知质量；2) 提出自回归展开与自适应上下文切换机制，缓解极长序列中的时间漂移问题；3) 提出重编码对齐与交叉残差校正，确保帧插值过程中精确的双向一致性；4) 提出事件体素密度增强技术，提升对不同传感器分辨率的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，LongE2V在事件视频重建、预测和帧插值三项任务上均优于当前最先进方法，展现出卓越的时间连贯性和零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: LongE2V通过引入视频扩散先验和多项专门设计的技术创新，有效解决了从稀疏事件流恢复高质量视频中的纹理模糊和长期不稳定问题，在多项任务上取得了最优性能。

Original Abstract: Recovering high-quality video from sparse event streams is a challenging task. Regression methods often blur textures, while existing generative models struggle with long-term stability. We propose LongE2V, a novel approach that leverages pre-trained video diffusion priors to jointly handle event-based video reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. By fine-tuning a foundational video model, our approach achieves high data efficiency and superior perceptual quality. We introduce Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to mitigate temporal drift in extremely long sequences. We also propose Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure precise bidirectional consistency during frame interpolation. Furthermore, Event Voxel Density Augmentation ensures robustness across varying sensor resolutions. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LongE2V outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all three tasks, exhibiting exceptional temporal coherence and zero-shot generalization. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/

中文摘要翻译: 从稀疏事件流中恢复高质量视频是一项极具挑战性的任务。回归方法常常模糊纹理，而现有的生成模型则难以保证长期稳定性。我们提出了LongE2V，一种新颖的方法，它利用预训练的视频扩散先验来联合处理基于事件流的视频重建、预测和帧插值任务。通过微调一个基础视频模型，我们的方法实现了高数据效率和优越的感知质量。我们引入了自回归展开与自适应上下文切换机制，以缓解极长序列中的时间漂移问题。我们还提出了重编码对齐与交叉残差校正方法，以确保帧插值过程中精确的双向一致性。此外，事件体素密度增强技术保证了模型在不同传感器分辨率下的鲁棒性。在多个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，LongE2V在全部三项任务上均优于当前最先进的方法，展现出卓越的时间连贯性和零样本泛化能力。

</details>


### [71] [ZipDepth: Bringing Lightweight Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Anywhere, on Any Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08771)
*Fabio Tosi,Luca Bartolomei,Matteo Poggi,Stefano Mattoccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipDepth是一个仅6.1M参数的单目深度估计轻量网络，通过可重参数化编码器-解码器结合大规模知识蒸馏，在零样本精度和部署效率之间实现了最佳平衡，达到50倍参数基础模型相近的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基础模型在零样本单目深度估计上表现优异但计算量过大，无法部署在嵌入式或移动平台；而轻量级替代方案仅在单域自监督范式下开发，在域迁移时性能严重退化。

Method: 设计了一个高效的**可重参数化编码器-解码器**结构，并通过**大规模多域训练集**上的**知识蒸馏**从基础模型中迁移知识，仅保留6.1M参数。

Result: 在5个基准测试上，ZipDepth在零样本精度和部署效率之间取得了轻量模型中的最佳权衡，以6.1M参数达到了比其大50倍的基础模型相近的精度，可在服务器GPU到功耗受限设备上实时运行。

Conclusion: ZipDepth通过紧凑设计与知识蒸馏成功弥合了轻量级模型与超大基础模型在单目深度估计上的精度差距，为边缘部署提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 单目深度估计通过基础模型取得了显著进展，实现了鲁棒的零样本泛化能力，但其计算需求使其远无法应用于嵌入式及移动平台。尽管存在轻量级替代方案，但这些方案几乎完全在单域、自监督范式下开发，在域迁移时性能会无声退化。我们提出ZipDepth——一个紧凑的单目深度网络，通过结合高效的**可重参数化编码器-解码器**与基于大规模多域训练集的**基础模型知识蒸馏**，弥合了这一差距。ZipDepth仅包含6.1M参数，可从服务器GPU到功耗受限设备上实时运行，在五个基准测试中实现了轻量级模型间最优的零样本精度与部署效率权衡，向着精度与参数多50倍的基础模型看齐迈出了重要一步。

</details>


### [72] [Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08772)
*Jiangwei Ren,Xingyu Jiang,Zijie Song,Wei Xu,Hongkai Lin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wat3R提出了一种无需水下标注数据的跨域半监督学习框架，通过教师-学生架构和跨视角一致性损失，将前馈3D重建模型从空气环境迁移到水下场景，并在新构建的Water3D数据集上实现了最先进的多视图深度估计和点云重建性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 水下环境存在光衰减、散射等问题，且缺乏大规模高质量3D标注数据，现有依赖密集标注的方法在水下场景中不切实际，因此需要一种无需水下标注数据即可实现高质量3D几何重建的方法。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构的半监督学习框架，仅利用大量无标注的真实水下视频数据学习鲁棒的几何表征；设计跨视角一致性损失，利用其他视角的几何线索补偿当前视角因水衰减和散射导致的信息退化；同时构建了覆盖多种水体和场景的Water3D数据集用于评估。

Result: 在水下多视图深度估计和点云重建任务上，Wat3R超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的无需水下标注数据的半监督学习框架能够有效将3D重建模型从空气环境迁移到水下场景，在多个水下几何任务上取得最优性能，相关数据集和代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .

中文摘要翻译: 在水下环境中估计3D几何结构面临着独特的挑战，包括光衰减、散射以及缺乏大规模高质量的3D标注数据。现有开创性方法依赖于大量密集标注，这在水下环境中是不切实际的。本文提出Wat3R，一种跨域半监督学习框架，旨在将前馈式3D重建模型从空气场景迁移至水下场景。独特的是，我们的方法无需任何标注的水下数据，采用教师-学生架构，仅利用大量无标注的真实水下视频素材即可学习鲁棒的几何表征。我们还设计了跨视角一致性损失，利用其他视角的几何线索来补偿当前视角因水体衰减和散射导致的信息退化。此外，考虑到缺乏全面的评估基准，我们构建了Water3D数据集——一个涵盖多种水体和水下场景的多样化数据集，专用于几何任务评估。实验结果表明，Wat3R在水下多视图深度估计和点云重建任务上均优于当前最先进的方法。数据集和代码已开源，地址为https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R。

</details>

### [73] [A Non-Hermitian Potential Well Formalism for Conscious--Preconscious--Subliminal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08302)
*Vasily Lubashevskiy,Ihor Lubashevsky*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于复值景观和类薛定谔方程的全局神经元工作空间（GNW）现象学模型，统一描述了感觉编码、注意和意识访问的动态过程，并再现了潜意识—前意识—有意识的分层结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全局神经元工作空间（GNW）理论缺乏一个统一的动力学框架来桥接感觉编码与意识访问之间的过程，动机是构建一个可计算的现象学模型，将早期感觉处理与高级意识的涌现联系起来。

Method: 在感知状态空间的希尔伯特空间上定义云函数编码高级表征；采用虚时非线性薛定谔型方程驱动动力学，其非厄米、非正规哈密顿量包含厄米部分（通过耗散局域化实现识别）和反厄米部分（通过空间扩散实现信息广播），并引入Lotka-Volterra型非线性项以保持范数并实现非局域交互。

Result: 模型再现了潜意识—前意识—有意识的感觉处理层级；意识访问以束缚态的涌现为标志，且仅当全局神经元工作空间（GNW）景观深度和自上而下注意程度均超过阈值时才会发生。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可解的动力学描述，在统一的动力学框架内将感觉处理、注意和意识访问联系起来，为理解意识的神经机制提供了新的理论工具。

Original Abstract: We propose a phenomenological model of the Global Neuronal Workspace (GNW) in which early sensory processing generates an effective complex-valued landscape governing the dynamics of high-level stimulus representations. This landscape provides a dynamical bridge between sensory encoding and conscious access, enabling both processes to be described within a unified framework. High-level representations are encoded in a cloud function defined on a Hilbert space over a perceptual state space, thereby combining the holistic structure of mental images with a neural implementation. Its dynamics is governed by a nonlinear Schrödinger-type equation in imaginary time with a non-Hermitian, non-normal Hamiltonian and a nonlinear Lotka--Volterra-type term that preserves norm and enables spatially nonlocal interactions. The Hermitian and anti-Hermitian parts of the Hamiltonian generate complementary processes: recognition via dissipative localization at minima of the GNW landscape and information broadcasting via spatial spreading across the state space. The resulting dynamics reproduces the subliminal--preconscious--conscious hierarchy of sensory processing. Conscious access corresponds to the emergence of a bound state, which occurs only when both the GNW landscape depth and the degree of top-down attention exceed threshold values. The resulting framework provides a tractable dynamical description linking sensory processing, attention, and conscious access within a unified dynamical setting.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个全局神经元工作空间（GNW）的现象学模型，其中早期感觉处理生成一个有效的复值景观，支配高级刺激表征的动态。该景观提供了感觉编码与意识访问之间的动力学桥梁，使得两种过程能够在统一框架内进行描述。高级表征被编码在定义于感知状态空间之希尔伯特空间上的云函数中，从而将心理意象的整体结构与神经实现结合起来。其动力学由一个虚时非线性薛定谔型方程支配，该方程具有非厄米、非正规的哈密顿量，以及一个保持范数并实现空间非局域相互作用的非线性Lotka-Volterra型项。哈密顿量的厄米部分和反厄米部分生成互补的过程：通过GNW景观极小值处的耗散局域化实现识别，以及通过状态空间上的空间扩散实现信息广播。由此产生的动力学再现了感觉处理的潜意识—前意识—有意识层级。意识访问对应于束缚态的涌现，且仅在GNW景观深度和自上而下注意程度均超过阈值时才会发生。由此产生的框架提供了一个可解的动力学描述，在统一的动力学设定内将感觉处理、注意和意识访问联系起来。

</details>

### [74] [Projected Energy Matching for Generative 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07749)
*Daniel Barco,Michal Balcerak,Suprosanna Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Philipp Denzel,Bjoern Menze,Frank-Peter Schilling*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出投影能量匹配（Projected Energy Matching）框架，通过亥姆霍兹蒸馏解决能量匹配中速度场非保守旋度分量与标量势的结构冲突，并引入负缓存策略实现高效对比训练，在医疗CT稀疏视图重建中取得高质量结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 直接在高维3D数据上训练能量匹配的标量势计算成本极高；从预训练流模型蒸馏虽然降低了初始训练成本，但流模型的速度场包含非保守的旋转伪影（旋度），迫使严格保守的标量势去拟合这种无约束场会产生"结构冲突"，损害生成质量和模式覆盖。

Method: 提出投影能量匹配框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 亥姆霍兹蒸馏——利用Hutchinson迹估计器将旋转噪声显式吸收到辅助残差网络中，实现结构松弛；(2) 负缓存——一种内存高效策略，在微批次间重用负样本，使得梯度累积下的对比训练中采样变得可行。

Result: 在真实世界医疗CT稀疏视图重建任务中，所提方法将总计算量降低到标准能量匹配所需的一小部分，同时实现高保真重建并成功解决严重的测量伪影。

Conclusion: 投影能量匹配通过结构松弛和内存优化，有效解决了能量匹配框架在高维3D数据上的计算瓶颈和结构冲突问题，为逆问题提供了高效的无条件先验。

Original Abstract: Energy Matching has emerged as a powerful generative framework that combines flow model efficiency with the explicit likelihood of Energy-Based Models (EBMs) via a single, time-independent scalar potential. However, directly training this potential on high-dimensional 3D data remains computationally challenging. While distilling a pre-trained flow model circumvents some of the initial training costs, we demonstrate that velocity fields inevitably contain non-conservative rotational artifacts (curl). Forcing a strictly conservative scalar potential to match this unconstrained field creates a "structural conflict", which degrades generation quality and mode coverage. To solve this, we propose Projected Energy Matching, a scalable framework that resolves these structural and computational bottlenecks. We introduce Helmholtz Distillation, a structural relaxation that leverages a Hutchinson trace estimator to explicitly absorb rotational noise into an auxiliary residual network. We subsequently refine this landscape using Negative Caching, a memory-efficient strategy that reuses negative samples across micro-batches, rendering sampling tractable during contrastive training with gradient accumulation. We deploy our method as an unconditional prior for real-world medical CT inverse problems, specifically sparse-view reconstruction. Ultimately, our amortized pipeline reduces total compute to a small fraction of that required by standard energy matching, while achieving high-fidelity reconstructions and successfully resolving severe measurement artifacts.

中文摘要翻译: 能量匹配（Energy Matching）已成为一种强大的生成框架，它通过单个与时间无关的标量势，将流模型效率与基于能量的模型（EBM）的显式似然性相结合。然而，直接在高维3D数据上训练该势函数在计算上仍具挑战性。虽然从预训练流模型进行蒸馏可以规避部分初始训练成本，但我们证明速度场不可避免地包含非保守的旋转伪影（旋度）。迫使严格保守的标量势去拟合这种无约束场会产生"结构冲突"，从而降低生成质量和模式覆盖。为了解决这一问题，我们提出投影能量匹配（Projected Energy Matching），一个可扩展的框架，用于解决这些结构和计算瓶颈。我们引入亥姆霍兹蒸馏（Helmholtz Distillation），一种结构松弛方法，利用Hutchinson迹估计器将旋转噪声显式吸收到辅助残差网络中。随后，我们使用负缓存（Negative Caching）策略精化该势能面，这是一种内存高效策略，在微批次间重用负样本，使得梯度累积下的对比训练中采样变得可行。我们将该方法作为真实世界医疗CT逆问题（具体为稀疏视图重建）的无条件先验。最终，我们的摊销式流水线将总计算量降低到标准能量匹配所需的一小部分，同时实现了高保真重建并成功解决了严重的测量伪影。

</details>


### [75] [Towards Robust Semantic Video Transmission over Block Erasure Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07823)
*Nargis Fayaz,Homa Esfahanizadeh,Matin Mortaheb,Jinfeng Du,Harish Viswanathan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义感知的神经联合源信道编码（JSCC）框架，通过空间域和特征域两种设计，在块擦除信道上实现鲁棒的视频传输。空间域方法利用块划分处理局部随机丢失，特征域方法通过特征划分实现语义恢复，两者在不同擦除模式下表现出不同的鲁棒性权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频传输系统在块擦除信道上容易受到数据丢失的影响，传统方法缺乏语义感知的鲁棒性处理机制，需要一种能够结合语义信息、在空间连续性和语义冗余之间取得平衡的联合源信道编码方案。

Method: 提出神经视频压缩框架，包含两种设计：(1) 空间域设计：将视频帧划分为块，采用均匀擦除和两级语义引导的非均匀擦除策略，实现局部化擦除处理和细粒度鲁棒性控制；(2) 特征域设计：对潜在特征进行划分，使缺失特征能够通过语义信息恢复，同时保持整体空间一致性。

Result: 实验表明，空间域JSCC擅长处理随机局部丢失，而特征域JSCC在分布式擦除场景下具有更强的鲁棒性，且在低丢失场景下能保持更好的保真度。研究揭示了空间连续性与语义冗余之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 空间域和特征域JSCC在不同擦除模式下各有优势，两者互补的鲁棒性特性为设计鲁棒的、任务感知的视频通信系统提供了重要指导。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates semantic-aware neural joint source-channel coding (JSCC) for robust video transmission over block erasure channels. We propose a neural video compression framework exploring both spatial-domain and feature-domain designs. In the spatial domain, video frames are partitioned into blocks, enabling localized erasure handling and fine-grained robustness control via uniform erasure and two-level, semantic-guided non-uniform erasure strategies. In the feature domain, latent features are partitioned, enabling missing features to be semantically recovered while maintaining overall spatial consistency. Comprehensive experiments quantify reconstruction quality under varying uniform and non-uniform erasure probabilities. Our results show that spatial-domain JSCC excels at handling random localized losses, whereas feature-domain JSCC provides superior robustness to distributed erasures and maintains fidelity under low-loss scenarios. The analysis highlights the trade-offs between spatial continuity and semantic redundancy, offering insights for designing robust, task-aware video communication systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究语义感知的神经联合源信道编码（JSCC），用于在块擦除信道上实现鲁棒的视频传输。我们提出了一种神经视频压缩框架，探索了空间域和特征域两种设计。在空间域中，视频帧被划分为块，通过均匀擦除和两级语义引导的非均匀擦除策略，实现局部化擦除处理和细粒度鲁棒性控制。在特征域中，潜在特征被划分，使得缺失特征能够在保持整体空间一致性的同时通过语义信息恢复。综合实验量化了在不同均匀和非均匀擦除概率下的重建质量。结果表明，空间域JSCC擅长处理随机局部丢失，而特征域JSCC在分布式擦除场景下具有更强的鲁棒性，并在低丢失场景下保持保真度。该分析揭示了空间连续性与语义冗余之间的权衡，为设计鲁棒的、任务感知的视频通信系统提供了见解。

</details>


### [76] [False Confidence: Automated Labels Confound Fairness Audits in Cervical Spine Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07852)
*Linus Juni,Aasa Feragen,Aditya Parikh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Automated segmentation of cervical-spine MRI is increasingly used in clinical workflows, yet no fairness audit exists for this anatomy. We show that auditing these segmentation tasks is complicated by a common property of modern segmentation datasets: expert-annotated gold labels are expensive, so abundant machine-generated (silver) labels are added to limit annotation cost. This matters because the reference used to judge a model can itself be biased. In this study, we present the first fairness audit of cervical-spine MRI segmentation across sex, age, and race using the CSpineSeg dataset. We observe that the deployed model is demographically fair, but the choice of reference label, however, is not neutral. Because a dataset's silver labels are generated by a model trained on its gold labels, any new model trained on those same gold labels agrees more with the silver labels than with expert truth: scoring identical predictions against silver rather than gold overestimates performance by ~8 Dice points and turns the fairness verdict for age from non-significant to significant - not by the gap inflation Parikh et al. report (which we term false magnitude) but by collapsing within-group variance (which we term false confidence). Reference-label provenance is thus a first-order confounder in segmentation evaluation: performance and fairness should be reported against expert labels, and any fairness claim stated together with the provenance of its reference.

### [77] [SCI-Mamba: Unsupervised Learning based Low-Light Image Enhancement for Non-Cooperative Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08033)
*Yiyong Sun,Weihang Shan,Shijun Wei,Diwei Zhou,Guang Zhai*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: SCI-Mamba是一个面向低光照轨道航天器观测的无监督增强网络，融合自校准无监督学习、线性复杂度VMamba架构与Retinex物理先验，并构建了专用的Space Dark-1.0数据集，在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度上优于CNN、Transformer及主流Mamba方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 太空图像存在严重的低光照退化问题，而配对正常/低光照空间样本极度匮乏，严重限制了监督式增强算法的泛化能力。现有方法难以在资源受限的星载硬件上实现有效的低光照增强。

Method: 提出SCI-Mamba框架，该框架融合了：(1)自校准无监督学习，无需配对数据即可训练；(2)线性复杂度的VMamba架构，适合资源受限的星载硬件；(3)Retinex物理先验，引导增强过程符合光照反射物理规律。同时构建了Space Dark-1.0数据集，包含真实轨道影像、暗室硬件在环测量和物理约束的合成数据。

Result: 与CNN、Transformer和主流Mamba方法进行全面比较，验证了SCI-Mamba在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度方面的优势。

Conclusion: SCI-Mamba为近距非合作空间操作提供了一种实用的低光照增强解决方案，兼顾了增强效果与计算效率，适用于星载硬件部署。

Original Abstract: Low-light visual perception acts as the core visual foundation for on-orbit servicing missions targeting non-cooperative spacecraft, supporting autonomous rendezvous, pose estimation, component detection and robotic capture operations. Spaceborne imagery suffers from severe low-light degradation, while the extreme scarcity of paired normal/low-light space samples severely limits the generalization capacity of supervised enhancement algorithms. To address this practical bottleneck, this paper proposes SCI-Mamba, an unsupervised enhancement network for low-light orbital spacecraft observations. The proposed framework unites self-calibrated unsupervised learning, linear-complexity VMamba architecture and Retinex physical priors, delivering a lightweight enhancement pipeline adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware. We construct Space Dark-1.0, a dedicated low-light spacecraft dataset integrating real orbital footage, darkroom hardware-in-the-loop measurements and physically constrained synthetic data covering diverse illumination, motion and attitude conditions. Comprehensive comparisons with CNN-, Transformer- and prevailing Mamba-based approaches verify the advantages of SCI-Mamba in visual authenticity, color fidelity and inference speed. The proposed framework provides a practical low-light enhancement solution for close-proximity non-cooperative space operations.
  The code is available at https://github.com/bitswh/SCI-Mamba

中文摘要翻译: 低光照视觉感知是针对非合作航天器的在轨服务任务的核心视觉基础，支撑自主交会、位姿估计、部件检测和机械臂捕获等操作。星载图像遭受严重的低光照退化，而配对正常/低光照空间样本的极端稀缺严重限制了监督式增强算法的泛化能力。为解决这一实际瓶颈，本文提出SCI-Mamba，一种面向低光照轨道航天器观测的无监督增强网络。该框架融合了自校准无监督学习、线性复杂度VMamba架构和Retinex物理先验，提供适用于资源受限星载硬件的轻量化增强流水线。我们构建了Space Dark-1.0，一个专用的低光照航天器数据集，整合了真实轨道影像、暗室硬件在环测量以及覆盖多种光照、运动和姿态条件的物理约束合成数据。与基于CNN、Transformer和当前主流Mamba方法的全面比较验证了SCI-Mamba在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度方面的优势。该框架为近距非合作空间操作提供了一种实用的低光照增强解决方案。

</details>


### [78] [ConRad: Efficient Conformal Prediction for Radiomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08084)
*Matt Y. Cheung,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ConRad框架，利用预测掩膜、输入图像、预测影像组学和边界不确定性等协变量，为分割导出的影像组学标量目标构建自适应共形预测区间，在保持覆盖的同时提升区间效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 影像组学特征常从模型预测的分割掩膜中计算，但分割模型可能过度自信或校准不良，导致衍生测量结果看似比实际更可靠。现有共形预测方法为分割导出的影像组学提供的黑箱区间忽略了测试时的图像外观、掩膜几何和分割不确定性信息，效率低下。

Method: 提出ConRad共形预测框架，针对标量影像组学目标，利用预测掩膜、输入图像、预测影像组学值和边界不确定性等协变量，构建自适应预测区间，同时保证边际覆盖。

Result: 在五个2D医学影像数据集和171个保留的影像组学目标上，ConRad相比基线方法在保持接近名义经验覆盖率的同时，显著提升了特征级区间效率。消融实验表明，分割边界不确定性特征对区间效率贡献最大。

Conclusion: ConRad能够利用多种协变量信息构建更高效的适应性共形预测区间，其中分割边界不确定性是提升区间效率的最关键因素。

Original Abstract: Radiomic features derived from medical images and segmentation masks are used to support decision making in clinical imaging pipelines. In practice, these features are often computed from predicted masks, but segmentation models can be overconfident or poorly calibrated, making derived measurements appear more reliable than they are. Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees, but black-box intervals for segmentation-derived radiomics can be inefficient because they ignore test-time information about image appearance, mask geometry, and segmentation uncertainty. We propose ConRad, a conformal framework for scalar radiomic targets that uses covariates derived from the predicted mask, input image, predicted radiomics, and boundary uncertainty to construct adaptive intervals while maintaining coverage. Across five 2D medical imaging datasets and 171 retained radiomic targets, we show that ConRad improves feature-level efficiency compared to baselines while maintaining near-nominal empirical coverage. Ablation results further indicate that segmentation boundary uncertainty features are the largest contributors to interval efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 从医学图像和分割掩膜中提取的影像组学特征被用于支持临床影像分析流程中的决策制定。在实践中，这些特征通常从预测掩膜中计算得出，但分割模型可能过度自信或校准不良，使得衍生的测量结果看起来比实际更可靠。共形预测（CP）提供了具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的分布自由预测区间，但针对分割导出的影像组学的黑箱区间可能效率低下，因为它们忽略了测试时的图像外观、掩膜几何形状和分割不确定性等信息。我们提出了ConRad，一个针对标量影像组学目标的共形框架，利用从预测掩膜、输入图像、预测影像组学值和边界不确定性中导出的协变量，构建自适应区间同时保持覆盖。在五个2D医学影像数据集和171个保留的影像组学目标上的实验表明，与基线方法相比，ConRad在保持接近名义经验覆盖率的同时提高了特征级效率。消融结果进一步表明，分割边界不确定性特征是区间效率的最大贡献者。

</details>


### [79] [Partial-Reference IQA Based on Hermite-Gauss Structural Prediction and Texture Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08563)
*Paolo Giannitrapani,Elio D. Di Claudio,Giovanni Jacovitti*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: PreSPA是一个部分参考图像质量评估框架，通过结构感知指数（无参考）和纹理敏感指数（仅需一个标量先验μ从参考图像中提取）两个互补指标分解感知质量，最终通过三个可解释参数的仿射融合得到评分，在六个基准测试中表现优于或媲美无参考和全参考方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法中，全参考方法需要完整参考图像，而无参考方法精度有限。作者希望提出一种介于两者之间的部分参考方法，在极小化参考信息量的同时达到接近全参考方法的精度。

Method: PreSPA将感知质量分解为两个互补指标：(1) 结构感知指数——通过Hermite-Gauss预测失真梯度场及其曲率角方差，以无参考方式捕获结构退化；(2) 纹理敏感指数——在强边缘区域通过参考与失真的复梯度图能量差异、在弱结构区域通过累积方式估计标量先验μ，反映退化边缘向周围纹理的感知泄漏。最终通过三个可解释参数的仿射融合得到最终评分。观看距离嵌入算子尺度中，无需数据集特定校准。

Result: 在六个标准基准测试上的广泛评估显示，PreSPA持续媲美或超越领先的无参考方法，在多个案例中达到与全参考模型相当的精度。

Conclusion: PreSPA是一种紧凑、透明且计算高效的部分参考图像质量评估方法，将参考信息减少到信息论最小值（仅一个标量μ），同时保持与全参考方法竞争的性能，具有良好的实用价值。

Original Abstract: We propose PreSPA (Partial-Reference Structural Prediction Approach), a Partial-Reference Image Quality Assessment framework that decomposes perceptual quality into two complementary indices. A structure-aware index, operating in a No-Reference manner, captures structural degradation through Hermite-Gauss prediction of the distorted gradient field and the angular variance of its curvature. A texture-sensitive index estimates local noise through a scalar prior $μ$, obtained from energy differences between reference and distorted complex gradient maps on strong-edge regions and accumulated over weakly-structured ones, reflecting the perceptual leakage of degraded edges into surrounding textures. Crucially, $μ$ is the only information extracted from the reference and is computed once per image pair, reducing the reference footprint to its information-theoretic minimum. The final score is produced by an affine fusion with only three interpretable parameters, making the method compact, transparent, and computationally efficient, with the viewing distance embedded into the operator scale and no dataset-specific calibration. Extensive evaluations on six standard benchmarks show that PreSPA consistently rivals or exceeds leading No-Reference approaches, while in several cases matching the accuracy of Full-Reference models.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了PreSPA（部分参考结构预测方法），一种部分参考图像质量评估框架，将感知质量分解为两个互补指标。一个结构感知指标以无参考方式运行，通过Hermite-Gauss预测失真梯度场及其曲率的角方差来捕获结构退化。一个纹理敏感指标通过标量先验μ估计局部噪声，该先验在强边缘区域通过参考与失真复梯度图之间的能量差异获得，并在弱结构区域上累积，反映退化边缘向周围纹理的感知泄漏。关键在于，μ是从参考图像中提取的唯一信息，且每个图像对仅计算一次，将参考足迹减少到其信息论最小值。最终得分通过仅包含三个可解释参数的仿射融合产生，使该方法紧凑、透明且计算高效，观看距离嵌入算子尺度中，无需数据集特定校准。在六个标准基准上的广泛评估表明，PreSPA持续媲美或超越领先的无参考方法，同时在多个案例中达到与全参考模型相当的精度。

</details>

### [80] [Entropy-Adaptive Multi-Map Chaotic Modulation for Physical-Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07828)
*Dhrumil Bhatt,Aarish Patel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种多映射动态熵感知混沌调制框架（MU-DE-IAEACM-MM），通过将熵作为可调控的安全变量并动态更新混沌控制参数，结合异构混沌映射与基于相关性的入侵检测指标，在多人无线系统中实现自适应的物理层安全通信。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于混沌的物理层安全方案依赖静态参数保密，难以应对自适应窃听攻击，且在多用户场景下存在统计依赖性高、安全容量受限等问题。为此，本文提出将熵作为可动态调节的安全变量，实现更具鲁棒性的自适应安全机制。

Method: 1) 采用熵驱动的自适应律动态更新混沌控制参数，将熵作为调节安全级别的关键变量；2) 在多用户间分配异构混沌映射（Logistic、Tent、Chebyshev和Sine生成器），以扩大熵维度并降低用户间统计依赖；3) 引入基于相关性的入侵检测指标，感知对抗性重建相干性提升并触发受控的熵递增；4) 进行稳定性分析，建立自适应更新过程的有界收敛条件。

Result: 在AWGN、衰落、脉冲、有色噪声和窄带干扰等多种信道条件下，合法接收机与失配接收机之间持续存在误码率差距，且安全容量显著优于传统的固定参数混沌调制方案。在最多64个合法用户和30个被动窃听者的密集部署场景下，仍能保持正安全速率。

Conclusion: 熵调节的多映射混沌调制为下一代无线和物联网网络提供了一种可扩展、同步稳定的自适应物理层安全方法，能够有效应对多用户环境下的窃听威胁。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a Multi-Map Dynamic-Entropy Intrusion-Aware Chaotic Modulation (MU-DE-IAEACM-MM) framework for adaptive physical-layer security in multi-user wireless systems. Unlike conventional chaos-based schemes that rely on static parameter secrecy, the proposed architecture treats entropy as a regulated security variable and dynamically updates chaotic control parameters using an entropy-driven adaptation law. Heterogeneous chaotic maps, including Logistic, Tent, Chebyshev, and Sine generators, are distributed across users to enlarge entropy dimensionality and reduce cross-user statistical dependence. A correlation-based intrusion metric is incorporated to detect improved adversarial reconstruction coherence and trigger controlled entropy escalation. Stability analysis establishes bounded convergence conditions for the adaptive update process. Monte Carlo simulations under AWGN, fading, impulsive, colored, and narrowband interference demonstrate a persistent BER gap between legitimate and mismatched receivers and measurable secrecy capacity gains over representative fixed-parameter chaotic modulation schemes. The framework maintains positive secrecy rates in dense deployments with up to 64 legitimate users and 30 passive eavesdroppers. The results indicate that entropy-regulated multi-map chaotic modulation provides a scalable and synchronisation-stable approach for adaptive physical-layer security in next-generation wireless and IoT networks.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种面向多用户无线系统的多映射动态熵感知入侵感知混沌调制框架（MU-DE-IAEACM-MM），用于实现自适应的物理层安全。与依赖静态参数保密的传统混沌方案不同，所提出的架构将熵视为一个可调控的安全变量，并利用熵驱动的自适应律动态更新混沌控制参数。异构混沌映射（包括Logistic、Tent、Chebyshev和Sine生成器）被分配至不同用户，以扩大熵维度并降低用户间的统计依赖性。引入基于相关性的入侵检测指标，用于感知对抗性重建相干性的增强，并触发受控的熵递增。稳定性分析建立了自适应更新过程的有界收敛条件。在AWGN、衰落、脉冲、有色噪声和窄带干扰条件下的蒙特卡洛仿真表明，合法接收机与失配接收机之间持续存在误码率差距，且与典型的固定参数混沌调制方案相比，可获得可观的安全容量增益。在多达64个合法用户和30个被动窃听者的密集部署场景中，该框架仍能保持正安全速率。结果表明，熵调节的多映射混沌调制为下一代无线和物联网网络提供了一种可扩展、同步稳定的自适应物理层安全方法。

</details>


### [81] [Optimal Multi-RIS Placement: Coverage-Guaranteed Sum Rate Maximization Under Inhomogeneous User Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07990)
*Abhishek Rajasekaran,Mehdi Karbalayghareh,Xiaoyan Ma,David J. Love,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层优化框架，在保证概率覆盖的前提下，用最少的可重构智能表面（RIS）最大化系统期望总和速率，兼顾公平性与吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RIS部署研究主要关注通过最大化覆盖来保障公平性，但未能利用用户密度的空间分布来提升吞吐量。本文旨在在保证公平性的同时不损失吞吐量。

Method: 首先，在可见性图上求解约束集合覆盖问题，得到可部署RIS的最优区域以提供覆盖保证；然后，在最优区域形成的交超图上采用贪心最小划分，得到满足覆盖保证所需的最少RIS数量；最后，基于贝叶斯优化计算最终的最优RIS部署位置。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提框架能够在无需不切实际的系统假设下，一致地找到同时实现良好覆盖和吞吐量的RIS部署方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分层非凸优化框架能够在不牺牲吞吐量的前提下保证覆盖公平性，且无需不切实际的系统假设，为RIS的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: The realization of the full potential of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) in a wireless system is tied to their strategic spatial deployment. While existing literature primarily focuses on enabling fairness by maximizing coverage to navigate through obstacles, these approaches often fail to exploit the spatial distribution of user density to maximize throughput. Thus, to enable fairness without loss in throughput, we formulate a novel hierarchical problem that maximizes the expected sum rate of the system while guaranteeing probabilistic coverage with the least possible number of RISs deployed. To solve this multi-layered non-convex problem, firstly, we obtain optimal regions where we can deploy RISs to provide the coverage guarantee by solving a constrained set-cover problem on a visibility graph. Then, the minimum number of RISs we require to satisfy the coverage guarantee is obtained by a greedy minimum partitioning on an intersection hypergraph formed using the optimal regions. Finally, a Bayesian Optimization based approach is used to compute the final optimal RIS placement. Numerical results are provided to show that the proposed framework consistently identifies placements that jointly achieve good coverage and throughput, without impractical system assumptions.

中文摘要翻译: 可重构智能表面（RIS）在无线系统中潜力的充分发挥依赖于其策略性的空间部署。现有文献主要关注通过最大化覆盖来穿越障碍以实现公平性，但这些方法往往未能利用用户密度的空间分布来最大化吞吐量。因此，为了在不损失吞吐量的前提下实现公平性，本文提出了一种新颖的分层问题，在保证概率覆盖的同时以最少部署的RIS数量最大化系统期望总和速率。为求解这一多层非凸问题，首先，通过在可见性图上求解约束集合覆盖问题，得到可部署RIS以提供覆盖保证的最优区域；然后，在由最优区域形成的交超图上采用贪心最小划分，得到满足覆盖保证所需的最少RIS数量；最后，基于贝叶斯优化的方法计算最终的最优RIS部署位置。数值结果表明，所提框架能够在不采用不切实际的系统假设的情况下，一致地找到同时实现良好覆盖和吞吐量的RIS部署方案。

</details>


### [82] [SpO$_2$ Predictor-Guided Stage-Wise Time-Frequency Reconstruction of Low-Quality Dual-Wavelength PPG for Oxygen Saturation Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07996)
*Zequan Liang,Elahe Hosseini,Ning Miao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Wei Shao,Ehsan Kourkchi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种SpO₂预测器引导的时频两阶段重建框架，用于低质量双波长PPG信号的恢复，通过联合时域-频域损失和SpO₂预测约束，在公开和私有数据集上取得了最低的MAE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PPG去噪和重建方法通常只优化波形保真度或心率特征，仅使用时域波形损失不足以保留频率结构和SpO₂相关信息，导致低质量PPG片段的SpO₂预测精度下降。

Method: 首先筛选高质量PPG段预训练SpO₂预测器；然后训练掩码重建模型，联合时域波形损失和基于短时傅里叶变换的频域损失恢复随机掩码区域；同时引入预训练的SpO₂预测器作为额外约束，使重建任务保留SpO₂生理信息；通过四个训练阶段联合优化SpO₂预测器和PPG重建器。

Result: 在公开OpenOximetry数据集上达到2.882%的MAE，在私有可穿戴PPG数据集上达到2.359%的MAE，均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的时频联合重建框架结合SpO₂预测器约束，能有效恢复低质量PPG信号并提升SpO₂估计精度。

Original Abstract: Continuous oxygen saturation (SpO$_2$) estimation from wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) is important for long-term health monitoring, but low-quality red and infrared PPG segments can distort waveform morphology and degrade SpO$_2$ prediction accuracy. Existing PPG denoising and reconstruction methods usually optimize waveform fidelity or heart rate characteristics, while time-domain waveform loss on PPG signals alone insufficiently preserves frequency structure and SpO$_2$-relevant information. This paper proposes a SpO$_2$ predictor-guided stage-wise time-frequency reconstruction framework for low-quality dual-wavelength PPG signals. The proposed method first selects high-quality PPG segments to pretrain a SpO$_2$ predictor. A masked reconstruction model is then trained to recover randomly masked PPG regions using a joint reconstruction objective that combines time-domain waveform loss with frequency-domain loss computed from the short-time Fourier transform (STFT). To make the reconstruction task physiologically relevant, the pretrained SpO$_2$ predictor is incorporated as an additional constraint, encouraging the reconstructed PPG to preserve SpO$_2$ information rather than only minimizing waveform reconstruction error. The SpO$_2$ predictor and PPG reconstructor model are optimized through four training stages. Experiments on the public OpenOximetry Repository and a private wearable PPG dataset show that the proposed approach achieves the lowest subject-level MAE, with 2.882\% on the public dataset and 2.359\% on the private dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 从可穿戴光电容积描记(PPG)信号中连续估计血氧饱和度(SpO₂)对于长期健康监测至关重要，但低质量的红光和红外PPG片段会扭曲波形形态并降低SpO₂预测精度。现有的PPG去噪和重建方法通常优化波形保真度或心率特征，而仅对PPG信号施加时域波形损失不足以保留频率结构和SpO₂相关信息。本文提出了一种SpO₂预测器引导的逐阶段时频重建框架，用于低质量双波长PPG信号。该方法首先选取高质量PPG片段预训练一个SpO₂预测器。然后训练一个掩码重建模型，利用联合重建目标——结合时域波形损失和基于短时傅里叶变换(STFT)的频域损失——来恢复随机掩码的PPG区域。为了使重建任务具有生理相关性，引入预训练的SpO₂预测器作为额外约束，鼓励重建的PPG保留SpO₂信息，而不仅仅是最小化波形重建误差。SpO₂预测器和PPG重建器通过四个训练阶段进行优化。在公开的OpenOximetry数据集和私有可穿戴PPG数据集上的实验表明，所提方法取得了最低的受试者级别MAE，在公开数据集上为2.882%，在私有数据集上为2.359%。

</details>


### [83] [Low-Rate Wrist SpO2 Estimation under Micro-Perturbations Using Motion-Aware Beat Selection and Perfusion-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08001)
*Zequan Liang,Ning Miao,Wei Shao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Ehsan Kourkchi,Hossein Sayadi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级低采样率腕式SPO2估计框架，通过运动感知的心跳选择与灌注引导校准，在微扰动条件下实现了准确的血氧饱和度估计，并降低了传感器功耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 腕式PPG信号的SPO2估计受到手腕微扰动和个体间局部灌注状态差异的影响，这些因素会破坏红光-红外比率(R)的稳定性，降低传统固定R-SPO2映射的可靠性。

Method: 从双波长PPG信号提取心跳级AC/DC分量，计算心跳级R值；利用加速度计导出的运动评分对滑动窗口内的心跳进行加权；使用受试者特异性灌注参考值在不同灌注条件下引导校准。

Result: 在25 Hz采样率下取得最优性能，MAE为2.305±1.113%，RMSE为3.117±1.743%；性能与100 Hz采样率相当，同时降低了PPG传感器功耗。

Conclusion: 提出的框架在微扰动条件下实现了低采样率腕式SPO2的有效估计，为能效优化的可穿戴实现提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Continuous oxygen saturation (SPO2) monitoring from photoplethysmography (PPG) is important for wearable health sensing, but wrist-based SPO2 estimation remains challenging due to subtle wrist micro-perturbations and inter-subject differences in local perfusion status. These factors can destabilize the red-to-infrared ratio-of-ratios (R) and reduce the reliability of conventional fixed R-SPO2 mapping. This paper proposes a lightweight low-rate wrist SPO2 estimation framework that integrates motion-aware beat selection and perfusion-guided calibration. The proposed method extracts beat-level alternating-current/direct-current (AC/DC) components from dual-wavelength PPG signals, computes beat-level R values, and uses accelerometer-derived motion scores to weight beats within each sliding window. A subject-specific perfusion reference is further used to guide calibration across different perfusion conditions. Experiments on a private wearable dataset show that the proposed method achieves the best 25 Hz performance, with an MAE of 2.305$\pm$1.113% and an RMSE of 3.117$\pm$1.743%, while maintaining performance comparable to the 100 Hz sampling rate and reducing PPG sensor power consumption for energy-efficient wearable implementation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for low-rate wrist SPO2 estimation under micro-perturbations.

中文摘要翻译: 通过光电容积描记术（PPG）进行连续血氧饱和度（SPO2）监测对于可穿戴健康感知至关重要，但腕式SPO2估计仍然具有挑战性，原因在于手腕微扰动以及个体间局部灌注状态的差异。这些因素会破坏红光-红外比值（R）的稳定性，降低传统固定R-SPO2映射的可靠性。本文提出了一种轻量级低采样率腕式SPO2估计框架，该框架集成了运动感知的心跳选择与灌注引导校准。所提方法从双波长PPG信号中提取心跳级交流/直流（AC/DC）分量，计算心跳级R值，并利用加速度计导出的运动评分对每个滑动窗口内的心跳进行加权。进一步利用受试者特异性灌注参考值在不同灌注条件下引导校准。在私有可穿戴数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在25 Hz下取得了最优性能，MAE为2.305±1.113%，RMSE为3.117±1.743%，同时性能与100 Hz采样率相当，并降低了PPG传感器功耗，实现了能效优化的可穿戴实现。这些结果证明了所提框架在微扰动条件下进行低采样率腕式SPO2估计的有效性。

</details>


### [84] [Unit-Independent Low-Rate Wrist GSR Processing for Stress Detection Using Phasic nSCR Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08007)
*Zequan Liang,Sally Hang,Geneva M. Jost,Ning Miao,Wei Shao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Hossein Sayadi,Ehsan Kourkchi,Setareh Rafatirad,Camelia E. Hostinar,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与单位无关的低速率腕部GSR处理流水线，通过提取每分钟皮肤电导反应数（nSCR/min）作为压力特征，在TSST与静息基线分类任务中达到0.823和0.871的平衡准确率，验证了低采样率、单位无关的腕部GSR用于可穿戴压力检测的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 腕部GSR的绝对幅值与实验室级别的掌部测量存在显著差异，导致传统的幅值依赖型处理方法失效，亟需一种与单位无关、可适用于低采样率腕部GSR的信号处理方案。

Method: 提出单位无关的低速率腕部GSR处理流水线：(1) 原始GSR信号清洗；(2) 分解为紧张性皮肤电导水平（SCL）和相位性皮肤电导反应（SCR）；(3) 鲁棒z-score归一化；(4) 检测相位性SCR峰值并计算每分钟SCR次数（nSCR/min）。使用随机森林分类器在25Hz We-Be GSR数据上进行二分类评估。

Result: 在TSST与坐姿基线、站姿基线的二分类任务中，nSCR/min分别取得0.823和0.871的平衡准确率；25Hz We-Be GSR特征与原始100Hz特征在各评估任务中表现相当。

Conclusion: 低采样率、单位无关的腕部GSR处理流水线可用于可穿戴压力检测，nSCR/min是有效的压力相关特征。

Original Abstract: Galvanic skin response (GSR) is widely used for stress detection, but wrist-based GSR remains challenging because its absolute amplitude can differ substantially from laboratory-grade palmar measurements. In this paper, we propose a unit-independent low-rate wrist GSR processing pipeline to extract the number of skin conductance responses per minute (nSCR/min) as a stress-related feature. We collect paired wrist and palmar GSR recordings from 31 participants during sitting baseline, standing baseline, neutral speaking, and the Trier Social Stress Test (TSST), a laboratory social stressor task. The proposed pipeline cleans the raw GSR signal, decomposes it into tonic skin conductance level (SCL) and phasic skin conductance response (SCR), applies robust z-score normalization, and detects phasic SCR peaks to compute nSCR/min. Using random forest on 25Hz We-Be GSR, nSCR/min achieved balanced accuracies of 0.823 and 0.871 for binary classification between TSST and the sitting and standing baselines, respectively. Moreover, the 25Hz We-Be GSR features achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the original 100Hz features across the evaluated tasks. These results suggest the feasibility of low-rate, unit-independent wrist GSR processing for wearable stress detection.

中文摘要翻译: 皮肤电导反应（GSR）被广泛用于压力检测，但腕部GSR仍具挑战性，因为其绝对幅值可能与实验室级别的掌部测量存在显著差异。本文提出一种与单位无关的低速率腕部GSR处理流水线，提取每分钟皮肤电导反应数（nSCR/min）作为压力相关特征。我们收集了31名参与者在坐姿基线、站姿基线、中性讲话和特里尔社会压力测试（TSST，一种实验室社会压力源任务）期间的配对腕部和掌部GSR记录。所提出的流水线清洗原始GSR信号，将其分解为紧张性皮肤电导水平（SCL）和相位性皮肤电导反应（SCR），应用鲁棒z-score归一化，并检测相位性SCR峰值以计算nSCR/min。使用随机森林在25Hz We-Be GSR上，nSCR/min在TSST与坐姿基线和站姿基线的二分类中分别取得了0.823和0.871的平衡准确率。此外，25Hz We-Be GSR特征在各评估任务中与原始100Hz特征取得了相当的平衡准确率。这些结果表明低速率、单位无关的腕部GSR处理用于可穿戴压力检测具有可行性。

</details>


### [85] [DKDNet: Dual Knowledge and Data-Driven Network for Cross-Domain Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08031)
*Shuang Wang,Chenxu Wang,Hantong Xing,Hanlin Mo,Lirong Han,Licheng Jiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合信号先验知识与数据驱动的双驱动网络（DKDNet），通过多表示特征编码器和动态轻量级融合单元，解决跨域自动调制分类中的分布偏移问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督域适应方法在跨域自动调制分类中仅对齐源域和目标域特征，忽略了在不同域条件下仍具有判别性的调制特定结构，因此需要引入信号先验知识来增强跨域表示学习。

Method: 首先分析了五种常用信号表示，从中选取IQ、AP和ACF三种先验引导输入；然后提出DKDNet，包含多表示特征编码器（MRFE）实现统一表示学习，以及动态轻量级融合单元（DLFU）实现自适应特征融合；最后通过调制分类和对抗域对齐目标联合优化融合特征。

Result: 在模拟数据集和公开数据集上的实验验证了先验选择的合理性，并证明了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 融合信号先验知识（IQ、AP、ACF）与数据驱动的方法能有效提升跨域自动调制分类性能，先验选择需综合考虑调制判别性、域稳定性和互补性。

Original Abstract: The dynamics of communication environments induce significant distribution shifts across domains, challenging the generalization of deep learning-based automatic modulation classification (AMC) models. While existing UDA methods alleviate this problem by aligning source and target features, they give limited consideration to modulation-specific structures that remain informative across domain conditions. In this paper, we consider signal prior knowledge, grounded in communication protocols and physical principles, as a potential way to enhance cross-domain representation learning. Given that different priors may vary in modulation discriminability, domain stability, and complementarity, this paper first analyzes five commonly adopted signal representations that instantiate different signal priors. From them, in-phase/quadrature (IQ), amplitude--phase (AP), and autocorrelation function (ACF) are selected as compact prior-guided inputs. Based on that, a dual knowledge and data-driven network (DKDNet) is proposed for cross-domain AMC. The multi-representation feature encoder (MRFE) and dynamic lightweight fusion unit (DLFU) are designed to achieve unified representation learning and adaptive feature fusion, and the resulting fused features are optimized with modulation classification and adversarial domain alignment objectives. Experiments on both simulated and public datasets validate the rationality of the prior selection and demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 通信环境的动态性会导致跨域分布漂移，给基于深度学习的自动调制分类模型的泛化带来挑战。现有的无监督域适应方法通过对齐源域和目标域特征来缓解这一问题，但很少考虑在不同域条件下仍具有信息量的调制特定结构。本文认为，基于通信协议和物理原理的信号先验知识是增强跨域表示学习的潜在途径。由于不同先验在调制判别性、域稳定性和互补性上存在差异，本文首先分析了五种常用的信号表示，从中选取同相/正交分量、幅度-相位和自相关函数作为紧凑的先验引导输入。在此基础上，提出了一种双知识数据驱动网络用于跨域自动调制分类，设计了多表示特征编码器和动态轻量级融合单元以实现统一表示学习和自适应特征融合，并以调制分类和对抗域对齐为目标对融合特征进行优化。在模拟数据集和公开数据集上的实验验证了先验选择的合理性，并证明了所提方法的优越性。

</details>


### [86] [Beyond Single-Band: Analysis and Resource Allocation for Multi-band ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08068)
*Haotian Liu,Zhiqing Wei,Xingwang Li,Yunxin Geng,Qixun Zhang,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多频段通感一体化（ISAC）系统的性能分析与资源优化框架，推导了多频段OFDM信号的检测与虚警概率闭式解，并设计了基于ADMM算法的联合功率与时频资源分配方案，在90%检测概率下相比传统单频段方案获得18 dB的检测增益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高精度感知需求与频谱碎片化现实驱动了多频段ISAC研究，多频段可通过不同频段的独立观测提供频率分集，减轻由频率选择性雷达散射截面（RCS）导致的感知性能波动，但缺乏系统的性能分析与资源优化方法。

Method: 推导了多频段OFDM信号的检测概率与虚警概率的闭式解析表达式，在此基础上构建了联合功率与时频资源的优化问题，并提出了基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的求解算法以最大化检测性能。

Result: 数值结果验证了闭式推导的准确性，证明多频段信号具有更优的鲁棒性。所提优化方案在90%检测概率下相比传统单频段基线实现了18 dB的检测增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的多频段ISAC性能分析框架与资源优化方案能有效提升检测性能与鲁棒性，为多频段通感一体化系统提供了理论依据与实用优化方法。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has emerged as a pivotal technology for sixth-generation wireless networks to empower high-precision sensing. The demand for superior sensing resolution and the reality of spectrum fragmentation have driven the research of multi-band ISAC. Multi-band ISAC provides frequency diversity through independent observations across disparate bands, mitigating sensing performance fluctuations caused by frequency-selective radar cross-section compared to single-band counterparts. In this paper, we propose a framework for analytical performance characterization and resource optimization in multi-band ISAC systems. Specifically, analytical closed-form detection and false alarm probabilities for multi-band OFDM signals are derived, providing a theoretical foundation for subsequent resource allocation. Then, a joint power and time-frequency resource allocation scheme is developed and solved via a proposed ADMM-based algorithm to maximize detection performance. Numerical results validate the accuracy of the closed-form derivations and demonstrate the superior robustness of multi-band signals. Notably, the proposed optimization scheme achieves an 18 dB detection gain over traditional single-band baselines at a 90\% detection probability.

中文摘要翻译: 通感一体化（ISAC）已成为第六代无线网络实现高精度感知的关键技术。对卓越感知分辨率的需求与频谱碎片化的现实推动了多频段ISAC的研究。多频段ISAC通过在不同频段上进行独立观测提供频率分集，相比单频段方案，能够减轻由频率选择性雷达散射截面引起的感知性能波动。本文提出了一个用于多频段ISAC系统分析性能表征与资源优化的框架。具体而言，推导了多频段OFDM信号的检测概率与虚警概率的闭式解析表达式，为后续资源分配奠定了理论基础。随后，开发了一种联合功率与时频资源的分配方案，并通过所提出的基于ADMM的算法进行求解，以最大化检测性能。数值结果验证了闭式推导的准确性，并展示了多频段信号的优越鲁棒性。值得注意的是，在90%的检测概率下，所提出的优化方案相比传统单频段基线实现了18 dB的检测增益。

</details>


### [87] [Low-Complexity Gridless Single-Snapshot DoA Estimation via Truncated Hankel Newton-MUSIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08108)
*Ruoxiao Cao,Wentao Yu,Yi Gong,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低复杂度的无网格截断汉克尔牛顿MUSIC框架，用于可重构天线阵列ISAC系统中的单快拍DoA估计，通过固定行维度的截断汉克尔矩阵恢复有效信号子空间，结合二阶牛顿更新减小量化误差，在保持高精度的同时显著降低计算复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在高动态ISAC场景中，DoA估计必须在短相干间隔内完成，通常只能处理单快拍数据。传统子空间方法面临秩亏问题，而基于汉克尔的空间平滑在大阵列时计算成本过高，亟需低复杂度的单快拍DoA估计算法。

Method: 提出截断汉克尔牛顿MUSIC框架：1) 构造固定行维度的截断汉克尔矩阵，降低相关矩阵构建和子空间分解的复杂度；2) 当截断长度独立于阵列大小时，主导复杂度与天线端口数呈线性关系；3) 在粗网格估计基础上，采用连续角度域的二阶牛顿更新来减小网格量化误差。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在DoA估计精度上接近方形汉克尔牛顿MUSIC，同时大幅降低运行时间。对于大阵列，相比传统方形汉克尔MUSIC，运行时间降低超过两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于可重构天线使能的ISAC系统中的实时感知，在保证精度的同时实现了显著的复杂度降低。

Original Abstract: Reconfigurable antenna arrays can provide enhanced spatial Degrees of Freedom (DoFs) for Integrated Sensing And Communication (ISAC) systems, enabling high-resolution Direction of Arrival (DoA) estimation. In highly dynamic scenarios, however, DoA estimation must be performed within short coherence intervals, which often restricts processing to a single snapshot. Conventional subspace methods then suffer from rank deficiency, while Hankel-based spatial smoothing incurs high computational cost when the array size is large. This paper proposes a low-complexity gridless Truncated Hankel NewtonMUSIC framework for single-snapshot DoA estimation. The proposed method constructs a truncated Hankel matrix with a fixed row dimension to recover an effective signal subspace while reducing the cost of correlation construction and subspace decomposition. When the truncation length is independent of the array size, the dominant complexity scales linearly with the number of antenna ports. To reduce grid-induced quantization errors, coarse grid estimates are further refined by a secondorder Newton update in the continuous angular domain. Simulation results show that the proposed method achieves DoA estimation accuracy close to square Hankel Newton-MUSIC while substantially reducing runtime. For large arrays, it provides more than two orders of magnitude runtime reduction compared with conventional square Hankel MUSIC, making it suitable for realtime sensing in reconfigurable antenna-enabled ISAC systems.

中文摘要翻译: 可重构天线阵列为集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统提供了增强的空间自由度（DoF），能够实现高分辨率的到达方向（DoA）估计。然而，在高动态场景中，DoA估计必须在短相干间隔内完成，这通常将处理限制在单快拍数据上。传统的子空间方法因此面临秩亏问题，而基于汉克尔的空间平滑在阵列规模较大时计算成本高昂。本文提出一种低复杂度的无网格截断汉克尔牛顿MUSIC框架，用于单快拍DoA估计。该方法构造了一个固定行维度的截断汉克尔矩阵，以恢复有效的信号子空间，同时降低相关矩阵构建和子空间分解的计算成本。当截断长度与阵列大小无关时，主导复杂度与天线端口数呈线性关系。为减少网格引起的量化误差，粗网格估计结果进一步通过连续角度域的二阶牛顿更新进行精炼。仿真结果表明，该方法在DoA估计精度上接近方形汉克尔牛顿MUSIC，同时显著降低了运行时间。对于大阵列，与传统方形汉克尔MUSIC相比，该方法实现了超过两个数量级的运行时间降低，使其适用于可重构天线使能的ISAC系统中的实时感知。

</details>


### [88] [Unified Analytical Model for Atomic Receivers Under Typical Quantum Interference Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08118)
*Yiyue Xiang,Neng Ye,Qihao Peng,Pei Xiao,Jianping An*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对原子接收器在量子干涉路径（V型、Λ型、Ξ型）上的信息论表征问题，提出了一种统一的分析模型。通过引入量子相干转移系数（QCTC）和稳态摄动框架，推导了三种路径的等效信道增益闭式表达式，为原子无线电通信的容量分析和波形优化提供了理论基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有原子接收器的信息论表征主要局限于Ξ型电磁感应透明路径，且依赖弱探针近似，无法预测高信噪比下原子接收器的行为，缺乏一个统一的理论模型来刻画不同量子干涉路径下的信道特性。

Method: 建立了一个统一的分析模型，应用于V型、Λ型和Ξ型三种典型量子干涉路径。基于三能级EIT解的稳态摄动框架，提出量子相干转移系数（QCTC）来建模原子接收器引起的等效信道响应，并推导了三种路径下等效信道增益的闭式表达式。

Result: 给出了三种量子干涉路径下等效信道增益的闭式表达式，为原子无线电通信中的容量分析和波形优化提供了分析基础。

Conclusion: 该统一分析模型克服了弱探针近似的局限性，为未来原子接收器在高信噪比场景下的性能评估、容量分析以及波形设计奠定了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Atomic receivers, which leverage the quantum interference termed electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) for radio-frequency (RF) to optical signal transduction, offer a revolutionary paradigm for next-generation wireless communications. However, current information-theoretic characterizations are predominantly restricted to the Ξ-type of EIT path and rely heavily on the weak-probe approximation, which fails to predict the behavior of the atomic receivers under high signal-to-noise ratio regimes. In this paper, we establish a unified analytical model for atomic receivers, and apply this model to three typical quantum interference paths, i.e., V -type, Λ-type, and Ξ-type configurations. To provide a universal characterization, we propose the quantum coherence transfer coefficient (QCTC) to model the equivalent channel response induced by atomic receivers, using a steady-state perturbation framework built on the three-level EIT solution. The closed-form expressions of equivalent channel gains are then derived for three paths. Our results provide an analytical foundation for future capacity analysis and waveform optimization in atomic radio communication.

中文摘要翻译: 原子接收器利用电磁感应透明（EIT）这一量子干涉效应实现射频（RF）到光信号的转导，为下一代无线通信提供了一种革命性范式。然而，当前的信息论表征主要局限于Ξ型EIT路径，且严重依赖弱探针近似，这无法预测原子接收器在高信噪比下的行为。在本文中，我们建立了原子接收器的统一分析模型，并将该模型应用于三种典型的量子干涉路径，即V型、Λ型和Ξ型配置。为提供通用表征，我们基于三能级EIT解的稳态摄动框架，提出了量子相干转移系数（QCTC）来建模原子接收器引起的等效信道响应，并推导了三种路径下等效信道增益的闭式表达式。我们的结果为未来原子无线电通信中的容量分析和波形优化提供了分析基础。

</details>


### [89] [GenAI-Enhanced Digital Twins for Predictive Interference Management in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08141)
*Afan Ali,Ali Arshad Nasir,Daniel Benevides da Costa*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于生成式AI增强的数字孪生框架，利用条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）合成罕见事件信道，结合最坏情况迫零波束赋形实现分布式鲁棒预编码，在超密集室内网络中实现了5-8 dB的SINR增益和60-70%的丢包率降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 超密集室内下一代网络中，由移动性引起的阻塞和局部多用户热点导致严重干扰，而传统数字孪生无法预测这些动态变化。

Method: 采用生成式AI增强的数字孪生框架，具体包括：(1) 使用带时空生成器和PatchGAN判别器的条件生成对抗网络(cGAN)进行主动式罕见事件信道合成；(2) 基于蒙特卡洛合成轨迹的最坏情况迫零(WC-ZF)波束赋形器，实现分布鲁棒预编码。

Result: 基于Sionna的仿真验证：中位SINR提升5-8 dB，丢包率降低60-70%，完美CSI理论性能差距缩小60-85%，推理开销仅为2.8-4.1 ms，控制信道开销约2.1 kB/10 ms时隙。

Conclusion: 该框架能以极低的控制开销和推理时延，显著提升超密集室内网络的抗干扰性能，大幅缩小与完美CSI理论性能的差距。

Original Abstract: Ultra-dense indoor next-generation networks suffer severe interference from mobility-induced blockages and localized multi-user hotspots that conventional digital twins~(DTs) cannot anticipate. We propose a generative AI~(GenAI)-enhanced DT framework employing a conditional generative adversarial network~(cGAN) with a spatio-temporal generator and PatchGAN discriminator for proactive rare-event channel synthesis. A worst-case zero-forcing~(WC-ZF) beamformer driven by Monte Carlo synthetic trajectories realizes distributionally robust precoding, with control-channel overhead bounded to $\approx$2.1\,kB per 10\,ms slot. Sionna-based simulations confirm a 5--8\,dB median signal-to-interference-plus-noise-ratio (SINR) gain, 60--70\% packet-loss reduction, and 60--85\% closure of the perfect channel state information (CSI) oracle gap within a 2.8--4.1\,ms inference overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 超密集室内下一代网络因移动性引起的阻塞和局部多用户热点而遭受严重干扰，传统数字孪生(DT)无法预见到这些情况。我们提出了一种生成式人工智能(GenAI)增强的数字孪生框架，采用带有时空生成器和PatchGAN判别器的条件生成对抗网络(cGAN)进行主动式罕见事件信道合成。由蒙特卡洛合成轨迹驱动的最坏情况迫零(WC-ZF)波束赋形器实现了分布鲁棒预编码，控制信道开销限制在每10毫秒时隙约2.1千字节。基于Sionna的仿真证实，中位信干噪比(SINR)获得5-8 dB增益，丢包率降低60-70%，完美信道状态信息(CSI)理论性能差距缩小60-85%，推理开销仅为2.8-4.1毫秒。

</details>


### [90] [Doppler-Resilient Rydberg Atomic Receiver for High-Dynamic Communication Networks via Adaptive Local Oscillator Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08145)
*Yiyue Xiang,Jianxiong Pan,Qiaolin Ouyang,Bichen Kang,Bin Qi,Neng Ye*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应本振跟踪的里德伯原子接收机架构，通过交叉乘积自动频率控制算法动态估计频率偏移并调整本振频率，使中频信号始终锁定在原子响应带宽中心，有效缓解高动态环境（如卫星通信）中的多普勒效应导致的性能退化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 里德伯原子接收机受限于窄瞬时带宽，在高动态场景（如卫星通信）中，严重多普勒效应导致载波频率偏移超出瞬时带宽，造成严重信号失真，亟需一种能锁定高动态信号的接收机架构。

Method: 提出自适应本振跟踪的里德伯原子接收机架构，采用交叉乘积自动频率控制（CPAFC）算法，动态估计瞬时频率偏移，生成误差控制信号，通过反馈环路调整本振频率，使中频信号始终锁定在原子响应带宽中心附近。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提架构显著优于现有里德伯原子接收机，有效缓解了高动态环境下的性能退化。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应本振跟踪架构能够克服里德伯原子接收机在高动态环境中的窄带宽限制，为下一代无线通信（如卫星通信）提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Rydberg atomic receiver has emerged as promising candidate for next-generation wireless communication, due to the exceptional sensitivity and ability to overcome the physical limitations of traditional radio frequency antennas. Utilizing the resonant response of atomic energy levels for signal detection, Rydberg atomic receiver is inherently confined to a narrow instantaneous bandwidth. However, in high-mobility scenarios such as satellite communications, the severe Doppler effect induces carrier frequency offsets, which drive the signal beyond the instantaneous bandwidth and result in severe distortion. In this paper, we propose an adaptive local oscillator (LO) tracking Rydberg atomic receiver architecture designed to lock high-dynamic signals within the effective atomic response bandwidth. By employing a cross-product automatic frequency control (CPAFC) algorithm, the system dynamically estimates the instantaneous frequency offset, generates a corresponding error control signal, and adjusts the LO frequency through a feedback loop. Consequently, the intermediate frequency signal can always be locked close to the center of the atomic response bandwidth regardless of dynamics. Simulation results show that the proposed architecture significantly outperforms existing Rydberg atomic receiver, effectively alleviating performance degradation in high-dynamic environments.

中文摘要翻译: 里德伯原子接收机因其卓越的灵敏度以及克服传统射频天线物理限制的能力，已成为下一代无线通信的有前景的候选方案。利用原子能级的共振响应进行信号检测，里德伯原子接收机本质上被限制在较窄的瞬时带宽内。然而，在卫星通信等高动态场景中，严重的多普勒效应会导致载波频率偏移，使信号超出瞬时带宽，从而产生严重失真。本文提出一种自适应本振跟踪的里德伯原子接收机架构，旨在将高动态信号锁定在有效的原子响应带宽内。通过采用交叉乘积自动频率控制（CPAFC）算法，系统动态估计瞬时频率偏移，生成相应的误差控制信号，并通过反馈环路调整本振频率。因此，无论动态性如何，中频信号始终能被锁定在原子响应带宽中心附近。仿真结果表明，所提出的架构显著优于现有的里德伯原子接收机，有效缓解了高动态环境中的性能退化。

</details>


### [91] [Fundamental Sensing Limits of 6G Cooperative MIMO-ISAC Networks: Joint Position-Velocity CRLB and Decoupling Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08510)
*Yanpeng Su,Norman Franchi,Maximilian Lübke*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对合作式MIMO集成感知与通信（ISAC）网络，提出了一种基于Cramér-Rao下界（CRLB）的性能边界分析方法。推导了包含位置与速度耦合项的完整Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）及精确CRLB，并提出了简化CRLB及其有效性判定准则，在降低计算复杂度的同时保证了可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究在合作式ISAC网络的CRLB分析中，大多忽略了Fisher信息矩阵中位置与速度之间的耦合项，导致性能边界分析不完整。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供包含耦合项的完整FIM及精确CRLB，并探讨简化的可行性。

Method: 首先推导了信号级参数到状态参数（位置和速度）的CRLB变换；然后推导了包含位置-速度耦合项的完整FIM及对应的精确CRLB，分别讨论了多单站感知、多双站感知及混合模式的情况；接着通过去除耦合项对FIM和CRLB进行简化，并给出了简化有效性的判定准则。

Result: 仿真结果表明，简化后的CRLB在大多数一般场景下均可适用；同时分析了资源和几何参数对位置和速度误差边界的影响，并通过CRLB表达式解释了简化CRLB的有效性条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化CRLB具有较低的计算复杂度，可为资源分配和波束赋形等优化问题提供可靠且易于处理的性能度量，且在一般场景下具有良好的有效性。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB)-based performance bound analysis of cooperative multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) networks. We first show the CRLB transformation of the signal-level parameters to the state parameters (position and velocity) in cooperative ISAC networks. Unlike existing studies that primarily ignored coupling between position and velocity in the Fisher information matrix (FIM), we derive the full FIM and the corresponding exact CRLB. Particularly, the results of multi-monostatic sensing, multi-bistatic sensing, and their hybrid are discussed. Addressing the complexity and tractability, we simplify the FIM and CRLB by excluding the coupling terms between the position and velocity, and provide a criterion for determining whether the simplification is valid. The simplified CRLB benefits from low computational complexity and provides a tractable and reliable performance metric for optimization problems such as resource allocation and beamforming. Finally, the position and velocity CRLBs and the simplification-induced error are examined in the simulation. The results demonstrate that the simplified CRLB can be applied in general cases. Based on the simulation results, the impact of resource and geometric parameters on position and velocity error bounds, and the validity of the simplified CRLBs is explained through the corresponding CRLB expressions.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对合作式多输入多输出（MIMO）集成感知与通信（ISAC）网络，提出了一种基于Cramér-Rao下界（CRLB）的性能边界分析方法。我们首先展示了合作式ISAC网络中信号级参数到状态参数（位置和速度）的CRLB变换。与现有研究大多忽略Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）中位置与速度之间耦合项的做法不同，我们推导了完整的FIM及对应的精确CRLB，并特别讨论了多单站感知、多双站感知及其混合模式下的结果。针对复杂性和可处理性问题，我们通过排除位置与速度之间的耦合项对FIM和CRLB进行了简化，并给出了判断该简化是否有效的准则。简化后的CRLB具有较低的计算复杂度，可为资源分配和波束赋形等优化问题提供易于处理且可靠的性能度量。最后，通过仿真检验了位置和速度的CRLB以及简化带来的误差。结果表明，简化后的CRLB在一般场景下均可适用。基于仿真结果，本文通过相应的CRLB表达式解释了资源和几何参数对位置和速度误差边界的影响，以及简化CRLB的有效性。

</details>


### [92] [RSMA-Assisted OFDM-OTFS Hybrid Framework for Mixed-Mobility Multiuser Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08532)
*Wafa Hedhly,Leila Musavian,Nikolaos Thomos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于速率分割多址接入（RSMA）的框架，用于未来6G车联网中OFDM与OTFS用户共存场景的下行通信。公共流帮助OFDM用户抵抗高多普勒效应引起的ICI，私有流与OTFS用户的流在不相交频段传输。仿真表明所提框架在中断概率和速率性能上优于正交复用方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 未来6G车联网中，采用OFDM和OTFS波形的用户可能在不同移动性条件下共存。OFDM用户在高移动性下会遭受严重的载波间干扰（ICI），而OTFS用户占用更大的频谱资源。需要一种灵活的多址接入框架来有效处理这些异构问题。

Method: 提出一种RSMA辅助的多用户下行通信系统。公共流承载OFDM用户的公共消息，覆盖整个带宽以帮助OFDM用户抵抗高多普勒效应导致的ICI；OTFS用户不参与公共流。OFDM用户的私有流与OTFS用户的流在不相交频段传输。所有接收端在SIC过程中考虑信道估计误差。

Result: 仿真结果展示了功率分配因子和信道估计误差对系统性能的影响，并证明了所提框架在中断概率和速率性能上优于正交复用方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的RSMA辅助框架能够有效处理6G车联网中OFDM与OTFS用户的异构共存问题，通过公共流设计帮助OFDM用户抑制ICI，在中断概率和速率性能方面均优于传统的正交复用方式。

Original Abstract: In future 6G vehicular networks, users employing orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) and orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) waveforms may coexist under diverse mobility conditions, where both can experience high-mobility and low-mobility profiles.
